                                <input type="text" id="transferDetails" placeholder="Name of new school or transfer reason">
                            </div>

                            <div class="btn-group">
                                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary" id="saveRecordSubmitBtn">ðŸ’¾ Save Record</button>
                                <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="resetRecordForm()">Reset Form</button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

    <!-- Toast Notification Container -->
    <div id="toastContainer"></div>

    <script>
        // Global records cache
        let allRecords = [];

        function getCSRFToken() {
            const input = document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]');
            if (input) return input.value;
            const cookieValue = document.cookie
                .split('; ')
                .find(row => row.startsWith('csrftoken='))
                ?.split('=')[1];
            return cookieValue || '';
        }

        function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
            const container = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
            if (!container) return;
            const toast = document.createElement('div');
            toast.className = `toast toast-${type}`;
            const icon = type === 'success' ? 'âœ…' : (type === 'error' ? 'âŒ' : 'â„¹ï¸');
            toast.innerHTML = `<span>${icon}</span> <span>${message}</span>`;
            container.appendChild(toast);
            setTimeout(() => {
                toast.style.opacity = '0';
                toast.style.transform = 'translateY(10px)';
                toast.style.transition = 'all 0.3s ease';
                setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
            }, 3500);
        }

        // Tab Navigation
        function activateTab(tabName) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));

            const targetContent = document.getElementById(tabName);
            if (targetContent) targetContent.classList.add('active');

            const targetBtn = document.querySelector(`.tab-btn[data-tab="${tabName}"]`);
            if (targetBtn) targetBtn.classList.add('active');
        }

        document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const tab = btn.getAttribute('data-tab');
                if (tab) activateTab(tab);
            });
        });

        // Toggle transfer input visibility
        function toggleTransferInput() {
            const select = document.getElementById('schoolTransfer');
            const inputGroup = document.getElementById('transferDetailsGroup');
            
            if (select.value === 'Yes') {
                inputGroup.style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('transferDetails').required = true;
            } else {
                inputGroup.style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('transferDetails').required = false;
            }
        }

        // Fetch records from API and refresh table
        async function fetchRecords() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/academics/api/records/');
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    allRecords = data.records;
                    renderTable(allRecords);
                    updateKPIs(allRecords);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Failed to fetch academic records:", err);
            }
        }

        function renderTable(records) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('recordsTableBody');
            if (!tbody) return;

            if (!records || records.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-light); padding: 30px;">No academic records found matching filters.</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            tbody.innerHTML = records.map(r => {
                const st = (r.promotion_status || 'Promoted').toLowerCase();
                let badgeClass = 'status-promoted';
                let label = r.promotion_status || 'Promoted';
                if (st.includes('not')) {
                    badgeClass = 'status-not-promoted';
                } else if (st.includes('cond')) {
                    badgeClass = 'status-conditional';
                }

                return `
                    <tr data-id="${r.id}">
                        <td>
                            <strong>${r.student_name}</strong>
                            ${r.admission_number ? `<br><small style="color: var(--text-light);">${r.admission_number}</small>` : ''}
                        </td>
                        <td>${r.academic_year}</td>
                        <td>${r.class_or_course}</td>
                        <td><strong>${r.percentage !== null ? r.percentage + '%' : '-'}</strong></td>
                        <td>${r.rank !== null ? r.rank : '-'}</td>
                        <td><span class="status-badge ${badgeClass}">${label}</span></td>
                        <td>
                            <div class="action-btns">
                                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="editRecord(${r.id})">âœï¸ Edit</button>
                                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="deleteRecord(${r.id})">ðŸ—‘ï¸</button>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        function updateKPIs(records) {
            let total = records.length;
            let promoted = 0;
            let conditional = 0;
            let notPromoted = 0;

            records.forEach(r => {
                const st = (r.promotion_status || '').toLowerCase();
                if (st.includes('not')) notPromoted++;
                else if (st.includes('cond')) conditional++;
                else promoted++;
            });

            document.getElementById('statTotal').innerText = total;
            document.getElementById('statPromoted').innerText = promoted;
            document.getElementById('statConditional').innerText = conditional;
            document.getElementById('statNotPromoted').innerText = notPromoted;
        }

        // Filter records locally or via input
        function filterRecords() {
            const search = document.getElementById('filterSearch').value.toLowerCase().trim();
            const year = document.getElementById('filterYear').value;
            const cls = document.getElementById('filterClass').value;
            const status = document.getElementById('filterStatus').value;

            const filtered = allRecords.filter(r => {
                const matchSearch = !search || r.student_name.toLowerCase().includes(search) || (r.admission_number && r.admission_number.toLowerCase().includes(search));
                const matchYear = !year || r.academic_year === year;
                const matchClass = !cls || r.class_or_course === cls;
                const matchStatus = !status || (r.promotion_status && r.promotion_status.toLowerCase().includes(status.toLowerCase()));
                return matchSearch && matchYear && matchClass && matchStatus;
            });

            renderTable(filtered);
        }

        // Open clean form
        function openNewRecordForm() {
            resetRecordForm();
            activateTab('add-record');
        }

        // Pre-fill form for editing
        function editRecord(id) {
            const rec = allRecords.find(r => r.id == id);
            if (!rec) return;

            document.getElementById('editingRecordId').value = rec.id;
            document.getElementById('formTitle').innerText = `Edit Academic Record: ${rec.student_name}`;
            document.getElementById('studentName').value = rec.student_name;
            document.getElementById('academicYear').value = rec.academic_year;
            document.getElementById('studentClass').value = rec.class_or_course;
            document.getElementById('percentage').value = rec.percentage !== null ? rec.percentage : '';
            document.getElementById('rank').value = rec.rank !== null ? rec.rank : '';
            
            const stSelect = document.getElementById('promotionDetails');
            const stVal = rec.promotion_status || 'Promoted';
            if (stVal.toLowerCase().includes('cond')) stSelect.value = 'Conditional';
            else if (stVal.toLowerCase().includes('not')) stSelect.value = 'Not Promoted';
            else stSelect.value = 'Promoted';

            if (rec.transfer_school) {
                document.getElementById('schoolTransfer').value = 'Yes';
                document.getElementById('transferDetailsGroup').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('transferDetails').value = rec.transfer_school;
            } else {
                document.getElementById('schoolTransfer').value = 'No';
                document.getElementById('transferDetailsGroup').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('transferDetails').value = '';
            }

            activateTab('add-record');
        }

        // Save (create / update) record via AJAX
        async function handleSaveRecord(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const btn = document.getElementById('saveRecordSubmitBtn');
            btn.disabled = true;

            const editId = document.getElementById('editingRecordId').value;
            const payload = {
                studentName: document.getElementById('studentName').value.trim(),
                academicYear: document.getElementById('academicYear').value,
                studentClass: document.getElementById('studentClass').value,
                percentage: document.getElementById('percentage').value,
                rank: document.getElementById('rank').value,
                promotionDetails: document.getElementById('promotionDetails').value,
                schoolTransfer: document.getElementById('schoolTransfer').value,
                transferDetails: document.getElementById('transferDetails').value.trim(),
            };

            const url = editId ? `/academics/api/records/${editId}/update/` : '/academics/api/records/create/';

            try {
                const res = await fetch(url, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken()
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    showToast(result.message || 'Academic record saved!');
                    resetRecordForm();
                    await fetchRecords();
                    activateTab('view-records');
                } else {
                    showToast(result.error || 'Failed to save record', 'error');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
            } finally {
                btn.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        // Delete record
        async function deleteRecord(id) {
            if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this academic record?')) return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/academics/api/records/${id}/delete/`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() }
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    showToast(result.message || 'Record deleted');
                    await fetchRecords();
                } else {
                    showToast(result.error || 'Failed to delete', 'error');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
            }
        }

        function resetRecordForm() {
            document.getElementById('academicRecordForm').reset();
            document.getElementById('editingRecordId').value = '';
            document.getElementById('formTitle').innerText = 'Enter Student Academic Record';
            toggleTransferInput();
        }

        // Initial setup
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            fetchRecords();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <!-- =====================================================
         JAVASCRIPT CONTROLLER & API INTEGRATION
    ====================================================== -->
    <script>
        // Global state
        let currentBenefit = "ALL";
        let searchTimeout = null;

        // CSRF Token Helper
        function getCsrfToken() {
            const name = "csrftoken";
            let cookieValue = null;
            if (document.cookie && document.cookie !== "") {
                const cookies = document.cookie.split(";");
                for (let i = 0; i < cookies.length; i++) {
                    const cookie = cookies[i].trim();
                    if (cookie.substring(0, name.length + 1) === (name + "=")) {
                        cookieValue = decodeURIComponent(cookie.substring(name.length + 1));
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            return cookieValue;
        }

        // Show Toast Notification
        function showToast(message, type = "success") {
            const container = document.getElementById("toastContainer");
            const toast = document.createElement("div");
            toast.className = `toast toast-${type}`;
            
            const icon = type === "success" ? "âœ…" : (type === "error" ? "âš ï¸" : "â„¹ï¸");
            toast.innerHTML = `
                <span class="toast-icon">${icon}</span>
                <span class="toast-msg">${message}</span>
            `;
            
            container.appendChild(toast);
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.add("show"), 50);

            setTimeout(() => {
                toast.classList.remove("show");
                setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
            }, 3500);
        }

        // Handle Year Change
        function handleYearChange(year) {
            fetchSummary();
            fetchRecords();
        }

        // Handle Benefit Filter from Stat Card
        function filterByBenefit(benefit, cardElement) {
            currentBenefit = benefit;

            // Update stat cards active style
            document.querySelectorAll(".stat-card").forEach(c => c.classList.remove("active-card"));
            if (cardElement) cardElement.classList.add("active-card");

            fetchRecords();
        }

        // Debounce Search
        function debounceSearch() {
            clearTimeout(searchTimeout);
            searchTimeout = setTimeout(fetchRecords, 250);
        }

        // Fetch Live Records from Backend REST API
        async function fetchRecords() {
            const year = document.getElementById("globalYearSelect").value;
            const search = document.getElementById("searchInput").value.trim();
            const schoolId = document.getElementById("schoolSelect").value;
            const status = document.getElementById("statusSelect").value;
            const rankOnly = document.getElementById("rankOnlyCheckbox").checked;

            let url = `/eligibility/api/records/?year=${encodeURIComponent(year)}&benefit_type=${encodeURIComponent(currentBenefit)}&school_id=${encodeURIComponent(schoolId)}&status=${encodeURIComponent(status)}&rank_only=${rankOnly}&search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`;

            try {
                const res = await fetch(url);
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    renderTableRows(data.records);
                    document.getElementById("recordCountLabel").textContent = `Showing ${data.count} records`;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Failed to fetch eligibility records:", err);
            }
        }

        // Fetch Live KPI Summary Counts
        async function fetchSummary() {
            const year = document.getElementById("globalYearSelect").value;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/eligibility/api/summary/?year=${encodeURIComponent(year)}`);
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    const s = data.summary;
                    document.getElementById("statTotalEligible").textContent = s.total_eligible;
                    document.getElementById("statBooks").textContent = s.books;
                    document.getElementById("statWorkbooks").textContent = s.workbooks;
                    document.getElementById("statStudyKits").textContent = s.study_kits;
                    document.getElementById("statLaptops").textContent = s.laptops;
                    document.getElementById("statInternships").textContent = s.internships;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Failed to fetch summary:", err);
            }
        }

        // Render Table Rows Dynamically
        function renderTableRows(records) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById("recordsTableBody");
            if (!records || records.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="8">
                            <div class="empty-state">
                                <div class="empty-state-icon">ðŸ”</div>
                                <h3>No Matching Eligibility Records</h3>
                                <p>No records found matching your current filter criteria. Try adjusting filters or execute the auto-evaluation engine.</p>
                                <button class="btn btn-engine" onclick="openEngineModal()">
                                    âš¡ Run Auto-Evaluation Engine
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
                return;
            }

            let html = "";
            records.forEach(r => {
                const firstLetter = (r.student_name || "S").charAt(0).toUpperCase();
                
                let benefitIcon = "ðŸ“š";
                if (r.benefit_type === "WORKBOOK") benefitIcon = "ðŸ“";
                else if (r.benefit_type === "STUDY_KIT") benefitIcon = "ðŸŽ’";
                else if (r.benefit_type === "LAPTOP") benefitIcon = "ðŸ’»";
                else if (r.benefit_type === "INTERNSHIP") benefitIcon = "ðŸ’¼";

                const rankHtml = r.selection_rank ? `
                    <span class="rank-badge ${r.selection_rank <= 3 ? 'top-3' : ''}">
                        ðŸ† Rank #${r.selection_rank} ${r.percentage ? `(${r.percentage}%)` : ''}
                    </span>
                ` : `<span style="color: #94a3b8; font-size: 13px;">â€”</span>`;

                const statusClass = r.eligible ? "status-eligible" : "status-ineligible";
                const dotClass = r.eligible ? "dot-green" : "dot-red";
                const statusText = r.eligible ? "Eligible" : "Not Eligible";

                html += `
                    <tr id="row-${r.id}">
                        <td>
                            <div class="student-cell">
                                <div class="student-avatar">${firstLetter}</div>
                                <div class="student-meta">
                                    <div class="student-name" onclick="openProfileModal(${r.student_id})">
                                        ${r.student_name}
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="student-sub">
                                        <span>${r.admission_number}</span>
                                        <span class="badge-gender">${r.gender || 'Active'}</span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <div class="school-name">${r.school_name}</div>
                            <div class="class-pill">${r.current_class}</div>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="benefit-tag tag-${r.benefit_type}">
                                ${benefitIcon} ${r.benefit_type_display}
                            </span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            ${rankHtml}
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <div class="status-pill ${statusClass}" onclick="toggleStatus(${r.id})" title="Click to toggle status">
                                <span class="status-dot ${dotClass}"></span>
                                <span>${statusText}</span>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <div class="reason-text" title="${r.reason}">
                                ${r.reason.length > 65 ? r.reason.substring(0, 65) + '...' : r.reason}
                            </div>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #64748b;">${r.checked_at || 'Auto-evaluated'}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td style="text-align: right;">
                            <div class="row-actions" style="justify-content: flex-end;">
                                <button class="icon-btn" title="View 360Â° Profile" onclick="openProfileModal(${r.student_id})">
                                    ðŸ”
                                </button>
                                <button class="icon-btn" title="Edit Record" onclick="openEditModal(${r.id}, '${escapeQuote(r.student_name)}', '${r.benefit_type}', '${r.academic_year}', ${r.eligible}, '${r.selection_rank || ''}', '${escapeQuote(r.reason)}')">
                                    âœï¸
                                </button>
                                <button class="icon-btn danger" title="Delete Record" onclick="deleteRecord(${r.id}, '${escapeQuote(r.student_name)}')">
                                    ðŸ—‘ï¸
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            });

            tbody.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function escapeQuote(str) {
            if (!str) return "";
            return str.replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
        }

        // Toggle Status via AJAX
        async function toggleStatus(recordId) {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/eligibility/api/toggle/${recordId}/`, {
                    method: "POST",
                    headers: {
                        "X-CSRFToken": getCsrfToken(),
                        "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    },
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message, "success");
                    fetchRecords();
                    fetchSummary();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || "Failed to toggle status", "error");
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast("Network error toggling status", "error");
            }
        }

        // Delete Record via AJAX
        async function deleteRecord(recordId, studentName) {
            if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to delete the eligibility record for "${studentName}"?`)) {
                return;
            }

            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/eligibility/api/delete/${recordId}/`, {
                    method: "POST",
                    headers: {
                        "X-CSRFToken": getCsrfToken(),
                        "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    },
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message, "success");
                    fetchRecords();
                    fetchSummary();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || "Failed to delete record", "error");
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast("Network error deleting record", "error");
            }
        }

        // Open Add Modal
        function openAddModal() {
            document.getElementById("recordModalTitle").textContent = "âž• Add / Override Benefit Record";
            document.getElementById("modalRecordId").value = "";
            document.getElementById("studentSelectGroup").style.display = "block";
            document.getElementById("studentNameDisplayGroup").style.display = "none";
            document.getElementById("modalStudentSelect").value = "";
            document.getElementById("modalEligibleStatus").value = "true";
            document.getElementById("modalRank").value = "";
            document.getElementById("modalReason").value = "";
            
            const currentYear = document.getElementById("globalYearSelect").value;
            if (currentYear && currentYear !== "ALL") {
                document.getElementById("modalAcademicYear").value = currentYear;
            }

            openModal("recordModal");
        }

        // Open Edit Modal
        function openEditModal(recordId, studentName, benefitType, academicYear, eligible, rank, reason) {
            document.getElementById("recordModalTitle").textContent = "âœï¸ Edit Eligibility Record";
            document.getElementById("modalRecordId").value = recordId;
            document.getElementById("studentSelectGroup").style.display = "none";
            document.getElementById("studentNameDisplayGroup").style.display = "block";
            document.getElementById("modalStudentName").value = studentName;
            document.getElementById("modalBenefitType").value = benefitType;
            document.getElementById("modalAcademicYear").value = academicYear;
            document.getElementById("modalEligibleStatus").value = eligible ? "true" : "false";
            document.getElementById("modalRank").value = rank || "";
            document.getElementById("modalReason").value = reason || "";

            openModal("recordModal");
        }

        // Save (Create or Update) Record
        async function saveRecord() {
            const recordId = document.getElementById("modalRecordId").value;
            const benefitType = document.getElementById("modalBenefitType").value;
            const academicYear = document.getElementById("modalAcademicYear").value;
            const eligible = document.getElementById("modalEligibleStatus").value === "true";
            const selectionRank = document.getElementById("modalRank").value;
            const reason = document.getElementById("modalReason").value.trim();

            if (!recordId) {
                // CREATE
                const studentId = document.getElementById("modalStudentSelect").value;
                if (!studentId) {
                    alert("Please select a student.");
                    return;
                }

                try {
                    const res = await fetch("/eligibility/api/create/", {
                        method: "POST",
                        headers: {
                            "X-CSRFToken": getCsrfToken(),
                            "Content-Type": "application/json",
                        },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            student_id: studentId,
                            benefit_type: benefitType,
                            academic_year: academicYear,
                            eligible: eligible,
                            selection_rank: selectionRank,
                            reason: reason,
                        }),
                    });
                    const data = await res.json();
                    if (data.success) {
                        showToast(data.message, "success");
                        closeModal("recordModal");
                        fetchRecords();
                        fetchSummary();
                    } else {
                        alert(data.error || "Failed to save record");
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    alert("Error saving record: " + err.message);
                }
            } else {
                // UPDATE
                try {
                    const res = await fetch(`/eligibility/api/update/${recordId}/`, {
                        method: "POST",
                        headers: {
                            "X-CSRFToken": getCsrfToken(),
                            "Content-Type": "application/json",
                        },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            benefit_type: benefitType,
                            academic_year: academicYear,
                            eligible: eligible,
                            selection_rank: selectionRank,
                            reason: reason,
                        }),
                    });
                    const data = await res.json();
                    if (data.success) {
                        showToast(data.message, "success");
                        closeModal("recordModal");
                        fetchRecords();
                        fetchSummary();
                    } else {
                        alert(data.error || "Failed to update record");
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    alert("Error updating record: " + err.message);
                }
            }
        }

        // Open 360Â° Student Profiler Modal
        async function openProfileModal(studentId) {
            const year = document.getElementById("globalYearSelect").value;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/eligibility/api/student-profile/${studentId}/?year=${encodeURIComponent(year !== 'ALL' ? year : '')}`);
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    const p = data.profile;
                    document.getElementById("profAvatar").textContent = p.student_name.charAt(0).toUpperCase();
                    document.getElementById("profName").textContent = p.student_name;
                    document.getElementById("profMeta").textContent = `${p.admission_number} â€¢ Enrolled: ${p.current_class} â€¢ School: ${p.school_name} ${p.percentage ? `(Academic Score: ${p.percentage}%)` : ''}`;

                    const grid = document.getElementById("profBenefitsGrid");
                    grid.innerHTML = p.benefits.map(b => `
                        <div class="benefit-profile-item">
                            <div class="benefit-profile-left">
                                <div class="benefit-profile-icon">${b.icon}</div>
                                <div>
                                    <div class="benefit-profile-title">${b.benefit_display}</div>
                                    <div class="benefit-profile-rule">${b.rule_summary}</div>
                                    <div class="benefit-profile-reason" style="color: ${b.is_eligible ? '#15803d' : '#b91c1c'};">
                                        ${b.reason}
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <span class="status-pill ${b.is_eligible ? 'status-eligible' : 'status-ineligible'}">
                                    <span class="status-dot ${b.is_eligible ? 'dot-green' : 'dot-red'}"></span>
                                    <span>${b.is_eligible ? 'Qualified' : 'Not Qualified'}</span>
                                </span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `).join("");

                    openModal("profileModal");
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast("Failed to load student profile", "error");
            }
        }

        // Open Engine Modal
        function openEngineModal() {
            document.getElementById("engineResultBox").style.display = "none";
            document.getElementById("btnExecuteEngine").disabled = false;
            document.getElementById("btnExecuteEngine").textContent = "ðŸš€ Execute Evaluation";
            openModal("engineModal");
        }

        // Execute Auto-Evaluation Engine
        async function executeEvaluationEngine() {
            const btn = document.getElementById("btnExecuteEngine");
            const year = document.getElementById("engineYear").value;
            
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll("input[name='engineBenefit']:checked");
            const targetBenefits = Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => cb.value);

            if (targetBenefits.length === 0) {
                alert("Please select at least one benefit program to evaluate.");
                return;
            }

            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.textContent = "â³ Computing Eligibility Matrix...";

            try {
                const res = await fetch("/eligibility/api/run-evaluation/", {
                    method: "POST",
                    headers: {
                        "X-CSRFToken": getCsrfToken(),
                        "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        academic_year: year,
                        target_benefits: targetBenefits,
                    }),
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    const m = data.metrics;
                    document.getElementById("engineResultSummary").innerHTML = `
                        â€¢ Processed <strong>${m.processed_students}</strong> active student records.<br>
                        â€¢ ðŸ“š <strong>${m.books_count}</strong> Books packages generated.<br>
                        â€¢ ðŸ“ <strong>${m.workbooks_count}</strong> Class 10 Workbooks allocated.<br>
                        â€¢ ðŸŽ’ <strong>${m.study_kit_top_count}</strong> Class 10 Merit Scholars & <strong>${m.study_kit_continuation_count}</strong> PUC continuers.<br>
                        â€¢ ðŸ’» <strong>${m.laptop_count}</strong> 2nd PUC Top 10 Laptop scholarships awarded.<br>
                        â€¢ ðŸ’¼ <strong>${m.internships_count}</strong> Degree Internship candidates identified.<br>
                        â€¢ Total Eligible Records for <strong>${m.academic_year}</strong>: <strong>${m.total_eligible_records}</strong>.
                    `;
                    document.getElementById("engineResultBox").style.display = "block";
                    btn.textContent = "âœ… Evaluation Complete";
                    showToast(data.message, "success");

                    fetchRecords();
                    fetchSummary();
                } else {
                    alert(data.error || "Engine execution failed");
                    btn.disabled = false;
                    btn.textContent = "ðŸš€ Execute Evaluation";
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert("Execution error: " + err.message);
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.textContent = "ðŸš€ Execute Evaluation";
            }
        }

        // Export CSV
        function exportCSV() {
            const year = document.getElementById("globalYearSelect").value;
            const schoolId = document.getElementById("schoolSelect").value;
            const status = document.getElementById("statusSelect").value;

            const url = `/eligibility/export/csv/?year=${encodeURIComponent(year)}&benefit_type=${encodeURIComponent(currentBenefit)}&school_id=${encodeURIComponent(schoolId)}&status=${encodeURIComponent(status)}`;
            window.location.href = url;
        }

        // Policy Modal
        function openPolicyModal() {
            openModal("policyModal");
        }

        // General Modal Helpers
        function openModal(modalId) {
            const el = document.getElementById(modalId);
            if (el) el.classList.add("show");
        }

        function closeModal(modalId) {
            const el = document.getElementById(modalId);
            if (el) el.classList.remove("show");
        }

        // Close on background click
        window.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
            if (e.target.classList.contains("modal-backdrop")) {
                e.target.classList.remove("show");
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                        <td>{{ record.academic_year }}</td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="badge">Eligible</span>
                        </td>
                        <td>{{ record.reason }}</td>
                    </tr>

                    {% endfor %}

                    </tbody>
                </table>

                {% else %}

                <div class="empty">
                    No students are currently eligible for internships.
                </div>

                {% endif %}

            </div>

        </main>

    </div>

</body>

                </div>

    <!-- Toast Notifications Container -->
    <div class="toast-container" id="toast-box"></div>

    <!-- =====================================================
         SCRIPTS & CLIENT-SIDE LOGIC
    ====================================================== -->
    <script>
        // State variables
        let allPlacements = [];
        let filterTimeout = null;

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            loadPlacements();
            loadCandidates();
        });

        // CSRF Token Helper
        function getCSRFToken() {
            const match = document.cookie.match(/csrftoken=([^;]+)/);
            return match ? match[1] : "";
        }

        // Toast Feedback
        function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
            const container = document.getElementById('toast-box');
            const toast = document.createElement('div');
            toast.className = `toast toast-${type}`;
            toast.innerHTML = `<span>${type === 'success' ? 'âœ…' : 'âš ï¸'}</span> <div>${message}</div>`;
            container.appendChild(toast);
            setTimeout(() => {
                toast.style.opacity = '0';
                toast.style.transform = 'translateY(10px)';
                setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
            }, 3500);
        }

        // Tab Navigation
        function switchTab(tabId, btn) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-pane').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
            document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add('active');
            if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
        }

        // Modal Management
        function openModal(id) {
            document.getElementById(id).classList.add('active');
        }

        function closeModal(id) {
            document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('active');
        }

        // Debounced Search Filter
        function debounceFilter() {
            clearTimeout(filterTimeout);
            filterTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
                loadPlacements();
            }, 300);
        }

        function resetFilters() {
            document.getElementById('filter-search').value = '';
            document.getElementById('filter-status').value = '';
            document.getElementById('filter-dept').value = '';
            document.getElementById('filter-program').value = '';
            loadPlacements();
        }

        // Fetch and Render Placements
        async function loadPlacements() {
            const search = document.getElementById('filter-search').value;
            const status = document.getElementById('filter-status').value;
            const dept = document.getElementById('filter-dept').value;
            const program = document.getElementById('filter-program').value;

            const url = `{% url 'internships:api_internships' %}?q=${encodeURIComponent(search)}&status=${encodeURIComponent(status)}&department=${encodeURIComponent(dept)}&program_id=${encodeURIComponent(program)}`;

            try {
                const res = await fetch(url);
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    allPlacements = data.results;
                    renderPlacementsTable(allPlacements);
                    renderEvaluationsTable(allPlacements);
                    document.getElementById('badge-placements-count').innerText = allPlacements.length;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error loading placements:", err);
            }
        }

        function renderPlacementsTable(placements) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('placements-tbody');
            if (!placements || placements.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: var(--text-light);">No internship placements match your criteria.</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            tbody.innerHTML = placements.map(p => {
                const initials = p.student_name ? p.student_name.split(' ').map(n => n[0]).join('').substring(0, 2) : 'ST';
                return `
                    <tr>
                        <td>
                            <div class="student-cell">
                                <div class="student-avatar">${initials}</div>
                                <div>
                                    <div class="student-name">${p.student_name}</div>
                                    <div class="student-meta">${p.school_name} â€¢ ${p.student_class || 'Scholar'}</div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <div style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--primary-dark); font-size: 13.5px;">${p.program_title}</div>
                            <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${p.company_name}</div>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="badge badge-dept">${p.department_display}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <strong style="color: var(--primary-dark);">â‚¹${p.stipend_amount.toLocaleString()}</strong><span style="font-size: 11.5px; color: var(--text-muted);">/mo</span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="badge badge-status-${p.status}">${p.status_display}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <div style="font-weight: 600; font-size: 13px;">${p.attendance_percentage}% Attd</div>
                            <span class="badge badge-grade-${p.performance_grade}">${p.performance_grade_display}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            ${p.certificate_issued 
                                ? `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary" onclick='viewCertificate(${JSON.stringify(p)})' title="View Credential">ðŸ† ${p.certificate_number || 'Certified'}</button>` 
                                : `<span style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-light);">Not Issued</span>`}
                        </td>
                        <td style="text-align: right; white-space: nowrap;">
                            <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick='openFastEvalModal(${JSON.stringify(p)})' title="Update Grade & Status">ðŸ“ Grade</button>
                            <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick='editPlacement(${JSON.stringify(p)})' title="Edit Placement">âœï¸</button>
                            <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="color: var(--danger);" onclick="deletePlacement(${p.id})" title="Delete Placement">ðŸ—‘ï¸</button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        function renderEvaluationsTable(placements) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('evaluations-tbody');
            if (!placements || placements.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align: center; padding: 30px; color: var(--text-light);">No evaluation records found.</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            tbody.innerHTML = placements.map(p => {
                return `
                    <tr>
                        <td>
                            <div style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--primary-dark);">${p.student_name}</div>
                            <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${p.project_title || p.program_title}</div>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <div style="font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;">${p.program_title}</div>
                            <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${p.company_name}</div>
                        </td>
                        <td style="max-width: 250px;">
                            <div style="font-size: 12.5px; color: #475569; line-height: 1.35;">${p.evaluation_feedback || 'No remarks recorded yet.'}</div>
                            <div style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--text-muted); margin-top: 2px;">Mentor: ${p.mentor_name || 'Assigned Lead'}</div>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <strong>${p.attendance_percentage}%</strong>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="badge badge-grade-${p.performance_grade}">${p.performance_grade_display}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            ${p.certificate_issued
                                ? `<span class="badge badge-status-COMPLETED">ðŸ† Certified (${p.certificate_number})</span>`
                                : `<span class="badge badge-grade-PENDING">Pending Completion</span>`}
                        </td>
                        <td style="text-align: right; white-space: nowrap;">
                            <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick='openFastEvalModal(${JSON.stringify(p)})'>Update / Certify</button>
                            ${p.certificate_issued ? `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick='viewCertificate(${JSON.stringify(p)})'>Preview</button>` : ''}
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        // Fetch and Render Candidate Pool
        async function loadCandidates() {
            const container = document.getElementById('candidate-grid-container');
            try {
                const res = await fetch("{% url 'internships:api_candidates' %}");
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success && data.results.length > 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = data.results.map(c => `
                        <div class="candidate-card">
                            <div>
                                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; margin-bottom: 8px;">
                                    <h4 style="font-size: 16px; color: var(--primary-dark); font-weight: 700;">${c.name}</h4>
                                    <span class="badge ${c.is_placed ? 'badge-status-COMPLETED' : 'badge-status-SHORTLISTED'}">
                                        ${c.is_placed ? 'Already Placed' : 'Available'}
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                                <div style="font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 4px;">
                                    ðŸ« ${c.school_name}
                                </div>
                                <div style="font-size: 12.5px; color: #475569;">
                                    ðŸŽ“ Class: <strong>${c.current_class || 'Degree Candidate'}</strong> â€¢ Adm: ${c.admission_number}
                                </div>
                                <div style="font-size: 11.5px; color: var(--primary); margin-top: 6px; font-weight: 600;">
                                    âœ“ Source: ${c.source}
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div style="border-top: 1px solid #f1f5f9; padding-top: 12px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                                <span style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${c.phone || 'Contact on file'}</span>
                                ${c.is_placed 
                                    ? `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" disabled>Placed</button>` 
                                    : `<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick='openQuickAllocModal(${JSON.stringify(c)})'>+ 1-Click Match</button>`}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `).join('');
                } else {
                    container.innerHTML = `<div style="grid-column: 1/-1; text-align: center; padding: 30px; color: var(--text-light);">No eligible candidates found in pool.</div>`;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error loading candidates:", err);
            }
        }

        // Program Auto-fill in Placement Modal
        function onProgramSelected() {
            const select = document.getElementById('p-program');
            const opt = select.options[select.selectedIndex];
            if (opt && opt.value) {
                if (opt.dataset.dept) document.getElementById('p-dept').value = opt.dataset.dept;
                if (opt.dataset.stipend) document.getElementById('p-stipend').value = parseFloat(opt.dataset.stipend);
                if (opt.dataset.company) document.getElementById('p-company').value = opt.dataset.company;
                if (opt.dataset.mentor) document.getElementById('p-mentor-name').value = opt.dataset.mentor;
                if (opt.dataset.email) document.getElementById('p-mentor-email').value = opt.dataset.email;
            }
        }

        function openPlacementModal() {
            document.getElementById('form-placement').reset();
            document.getElementById('p-id').value = '';
            document.getElementById('modal-placement-title').innerHTML = `<span>âž•</span> Add Internship Placement`;
            document.getElementById('p-student').disabled = false;
            document.getElementById('p-start-date').valueAsDate = new Date();
            openModal('modal-placement');
        }

        function openPlacementModalForProgram(progId) {
            openPlacementModal();
            document.getElementById('p-program').value = progId;
            onProgramSelected();
        }

        function editPlacement(p) {
            document.getElementById('p-id').value = p.id;
            document.getElementById('modal-placement-title').innerHTML = `<span>âœï¸</span> Edit Internship Placement`;
            document.getElementById('p-student').value = p.student_id;
            document.getElementById('p-student').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('p-program').value = p.program_id || '';
            document.getElementById('p-dept').value = p.department;
            document.getElementById('p-company').value = p.company_name;
            document.getElementById('p-project').value = p.project_title;
            document.getElementById('p-stipend').value = p.stipend_amount;
            document.getElementById('p-year').value = p.academic_year;
            document.getElementById('p-start-date').value = p.start_date;
            document.getElementById('p-end-date').value = p.end_date;
            document.getElementById('p-mentor-name').value = p.mentor_name;
            document.getElementById('p-mentor-email').value = p.mentor_email;
            document.getElementById('p-status').value = p.status;
            document.getElementById('p-grade').value = p.performance_grade;
            document.getElementById('p-feedback').value = p.evaluation_feedback;
            openModal('modal-placement');
        }

        async function savePlacement(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const id = document.getElementById('p-id').value;
            const payload = {
                student_id: document.getElementById('p-student').value,
                program_id: document.getElementById('p-program').value || null,
                department: document.getElementById('p-dept').value,
                company_name: document.getElementById('p-company').value,
                project_title: document.getElementById('p-project').value,
                stipend_amount: document.getElementById('p-stipend').value,
                academic_year: document.getElementById('p-year').value,
                start_date: document.getElementById('p-start-date').value,
                end_date: document.getElementById('p-end-date').value || null,
                mentor_name: document.getElementById('p-mentor-name').value,
                mentor_email: document.getElementById('p-mentor-email').value,
                status: document.getElementById('p-status').value,
                performance_grade: document.getElementById('p-grade').value,
                evaluation_feedback: document.getElementById('p-feedback').value,
            };

            const url = id 
                ? `/internships/api/internships/${id}/update/` 
                : `{% url 'internships:api_create_internship' %}`;

            try {
                const res = await fetch(url, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken(),
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message || "Placement saved successfully!");
                    closeModal('modal-placement');
                    loadPlacements();
                    loadCandidates();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || "Failed to save placement.", "error");
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Save placement error:", err);
                showToast("Server error saving placement.", "error");
            }
        }

        async function deletePlacement(id) {
            if (!confirm("Are you sure you want to remove this internship placement record?")) return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/internships/api/internships/${id}/delete/`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken(),
                    }
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast("Placement removed successfully.");
                    loadPlacements();
                    loadCandidates();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || "Failed to delete placement.", "error");
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Delete placement error:", err);
            }
        }

        // Fast Evaluation & Certification Modal
        function openFastEvalModal(p) {
            document.getElementById('eval-p-id').value = p.id;
            document.getElementById('eval-student-name').innerText = `${p.student_name} (${p.admission_number || 'N/A'})`;
            document.getElementById('eval-project-name').innerText = `${p.program_title} â€¢ ${p.company_name}`;
            document.getElementById('eval-status').value = p.status;
            document.getElementById('eval-grade').value = p.performance_grade;
            document.getElementById('eval-feedback').value = p.evaluation_feedback || '';
            document.getElementById('eval-issue-cert').checked = p.certificate_issued;
            openModal('modal-eval');
        }

        async function saveFastEvaluation(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const id = document.getElementById('eval-p-id').value;
            const payload = {
                status: document.getElementById('eval-status').value,
                performance_grade: document.getElementById('eval-grade').value,
                evaluation_feedback: document.getElementById('eval-feedback').value,
                issue_certificate: document.getElementById('eval-issue-cert').checked,
            };

            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/internships/api/internships/${id}/status/`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken(),
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message || "Evaluation & Certification updated!");
                    closeModal('modal-eval');
                    loadPlacements();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || "Failed to update evaluation.", "error");
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Update eval error:", err);
                showToast("Server error updating evaluation.", "error");
            }
        }

        // Program Track Modal & Creation
        function openProgramModal() {
            document.getElementById('form-program').reset();
            document.getElementById('prog-id').value = '';
            document.getElementById('modal-program-title').innerHTML = `<span>ðŸ¢</span> Create Internship Track`;
            openModal('modal-program');
        }

        async function saveProgram(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const id = document.getElementById('prog-id').value;
            const payload = {
                title: document.getElementById('prog-title').value,
                program_code: document.getElementById('prog-code').value,
                company_name: document.getElementById('prog-company').value,
                department: document.getElementById('prog-dept').value,
                location: document.getElementById('prog-location').value,
                duration_months: document.getElementById('prog-duration').value,
                stipend_amount: document.getElementById('prog-stipend').value,
                total_slots: document.getElementById('prog-slots').value,
                status: document.getElementById('prog-status').value,
                mentor_in_charge: document.getElementById('prog-mentor').value,
                mentor_contact: document.getElementById('prog-contact').value,
                eligibility_criteria: document.getElementById('prog-criteria').value,
                description: document.getElementById('prog-desc').value,
            };

            const url = id 
                ? `/internships/api/programs/${id}/update/` 
                : `{% url 'internships:api_create_program' %}`;

            try {
                const res = await fetch(url, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken(),
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message || "Program track saved successfully!");
                    closeModal('modal-program');
                    window.location.reload();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || "Failed to save track.", "error");
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Save program error:", err);
                showToast("Server error saving program.", "error");
            }
        }

        // 1-Click Candidate Match Modal
        function openQuickAllocModal(c) {
            document.getElementById('alloc-student-id').value = c.id;
            document.getElementById('alloc-student-name').innerText = c.name;
            document.getElementById('alloc-student-school').innerText = `${c.school_name} â€¢ ${c.current_class || 'Degree'}`;
            openModal('modal-quick-alloc');
        }

        async function saveQuickAllocation(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const studentId = document.getElementById('alloc-student-id').value;
            const programId = document.getElementById('alloc-program-id').value;

            try {
                const res = await fetch("{% url 'internships:api_quick_assign' %}", {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken(),
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ student_id: studentId, program_id: programId }),
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message || "Scholar allocated successfully!");
                    closeModal('modal-quick-alloc');
                    loadPlacements();
                    loadCandidates();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || "Failed to allocate scholar.", "error");
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Quick assign error:", err);
                showToast("Server error assigning scholar.", "error");
            }
        }

        // Certificate Preview Modal
        function viewCertificate(p) {
            document.getElementById('cert-view-name').innerText = p.student_name || 'STUDENT SCHOLAR';
            document.getElementById('cert-view-school').innerText = p.school_name || 'Partner Educational Institution';
            document.getElementById('cert-view-project').innerText = `${p.project_title || p.program_title} (${p.department_display})`;

            openModal('modal-cert');
        }
    </script>

                                <option value="EVENT_MATERIALS">Event Materials</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div class="form-group">
                            <label for="modalItemUnit">Unit of Measurement</label>
                            <input type="text" id="modalItemUnit" class="form-control" value="Pieces" placeholder="e.g. Pieces, Sets, Boxes, Units">
                        </div>
                        <div class="form-group">
                            <label for="modalInitialStock">Initial Stock Qty</label>
                            <input type="number" id="modalInitialStock" class="form-control" min="0" value="0">
                        </div>
                        <div class="form-group">
                            <label for="modalLowThreshold">Low Stock Alert Threshold</label>
                            <input type="number" id="modalLowThreshold" class="form-control" min="1" value="10">
                        </div>
                        <div class="form-group">
                            <label for="modalUnitCost">Estimated Unit Cost (â‚¹)</label>
                            <input type="number" id="modalUnitCost" class="form-control" step="0.01" min="0" value="0.00">
                        </div>
                        <div class="form-group full-width">
                            <label for="modalLocation">Storage Location / Rack</label>
                            <input type="text" id="modalLocation" class="form-control" placeholder="e.g. Warehouse A - Rack 4">
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-footer">
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal('addItemModal')">Cancel</button>
                    <button type="submit" id="addItemSubmitBtn" class="btn btn-primary">Save Item</button>
