    <!-- Toast Notification -->
    <div id="toastNotification" class="toast">
        <span id="toastIcon">â„¹ï¸</span>
        <span id="toastMessage">Message</span>
    </div>

    <!-- =========================================================
         JAVASCRIPT LOGIC
    ========================================================== -->
    <script>
        let allItems = [];
        let allLaptops = [];
        let allStudyKits = [];
        let currentCategoryFilter = '';

        function getCSRFToken() {
            const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(row => row.startsWith('csrftoken='));
            return cookie ? cookie.split('=')[1] : '';
        }

        function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toastNotification');
            const msgSpan = document.getElementById('toastMessage');
            const iconSpan = document.getElementById('toastIcon');

            toast.className = 'toast toast-' + type;
            msgSpan.innerText = message;
            iconSpan.innerText = type === 'success' ? 'âœ…' : (type === 'error' ? 'âŒ' : 'âš ï¸');

            toast.style.display = 'flex';
            setTimeout(() => { toast.style.display = 'none'; }, 4000);
        }

        function switchTab(tabId, btnElement) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));

            document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add('active');
            if (btnElement) btnElement.classList.add('active');

            if (tabId === 'books-tab') loadBooksTab();
            if (tabId === 'workbooks-tab') loadWorkbooksTab();
            if (tabId === 'study-kits-tab') loadStudyKitsTab();
            if (tabId === 'laptops-tab') loadLaptopsTab();
            if (tabId === 'essentials-tab') loadEssentialsTab();
            if (tabId === 'alerts-tab') fetchLowStockAlerts();
            if (tabId === 'ledger-tab') fetchLedgerTransactions();
            if (tabId === 'reports-tab') loadReportsTab();
        }

        function openModal(modalId) { document.getElementById(modalId).classList.add('show'); }
        function closeModal(modalId) { document.getElementById(modalId).classList.remove('show'); }

        // =========================================================
        // INVENTORY ITEMS (TAB 1)
        // =========================================================
        async function fetchItems() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/inventory/api/items/');
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    allItems = data.items;
                    renderInventoryTable(allItems);
                    updateKPICounters();
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Error fetching inventory items:', err);
            }
        }

        function renderInventoryTable(items) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('inventoryTableBody');
            if (!items || items.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="9" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-light); padding: 40px;">No inventory items match your search.</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            tbody.innerHTML = items.map(item => {
                let statusBadge = '';
                if (item.stock_status === 'OUT_OF_STOCK') statusBadge = `<span class="status-badge status-out-stock">Out of Stock</span>`;
                else if (item.is_low_stock) statusBadge = `<span class="status-badge status-low-stock">âš ï¸ Low Stock (â‰¤${item.low_stock_threshold})</span>`;
                else statusBadge = `<span class="status-badge status-in-stock">In Stock</span>`;

                return `
                    <tr data-category="${item.category}" data-status="${item.stock_status}">
                        <td><strong>${escapeHTML(item.item_name)}</strong><br><small style="color: var(--text-light); font-family: monospace;">${escapeHTML(item.sku)}</small></td>
                        <td><span class="category-tag ${item.category}">${escapeHTML(item.category_display)}</span></td>
                        <td><strong style="font-size: 15px;">${item.current_stock}</strong></td>
                        <td>${escapeHTML(item.unit)}</td>
                        <td>${item.low_stock_threshold}</td>
                        <td>${escapeHTML(item.location || '-')}</td>
                        <td>â‚¹${item.unit_cost.toFixed(2)}</td>
                        <td>${statusBadge}</td>
                        <td>
                            <div class="action-group">
                                <button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="openStockInModal('${item.id}', '${escapeJS(item.item_name)}', '${escapeJS(item.unit)}')">ðŸ“¥ In</button>
                                <button class="btn btn-warning btn-sm" onclick="openStockOutModal('${item.id}', '${escapeJS(item.item_name)}', '${item.current_stock}', '${escapeJS(item.unit)}')">ðŸ“¤ Out</button>
                                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="openEditModal('${item.id}')">âœï¸</button>
                                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="deleteItem('${item.id}', '${escapeJS(item.item_name)}')">ðŸ—‘ï¸</button>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        function filterCategory(category, btnElement) {
            currentCategoryFilter = category;
            document.querySelectorAll('.pill-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
            if (btnElement) btnElement.classList.add('active');
            filterItems();
        }

        function filterItems() {
            const query = (document.getElementById('itemSearchInput').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
            const statusFilter = document.getElementById('itemStatusFilter').value;

            const filtered = allItems.filter(item => {
                const matchCategory = !currentCategoryFilter || item.category === currentCategoryFilter;
                const matchStatus = !statusFilter || item.stock_status === statusFilter;
                const matchQuery = !query || item.item_name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || item.sku.toLowerCase().includes(query) || (item.location && item.location.toLowerCase().includes(query));
                return matchCategory && matchStatus && matchQuery;
            });
            renderInventoryTable(filtered);
        }

        function updateKPICounters() {
            document.getElementById('kpiTotalItems').innerText = allItems.length;
            document.getElementById('kpiTotalUnits').innerText = allItems.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.current_stock, 0);
            const lowStockCount = allItems.filter(i => i.is_low_stock).length;
            document.getElementById('kpiLowStock').innerText = lowStockCount;

            const badge = document.getElementById('tabAlertBadge');
            if (badge) {
                badge.innerText = lowStockCount;
                badge.style.display = lowStockCount > 0 ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
            }
        }

        function openAddItemModal() { document.getElementById('addItemForm').reset(); openModal('addItemModal'); }

        async function handleCreateItem(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const btn = document.getElementById('addItemSubmitBtn');
            btn.disabled = true;

            const payload = {
                item_name: document.getElementById('modalItemName').value.trim(),
                sku: document.getElementById('modalItemSku').value.trim(),
                category: document.getElementById('modalItemCategory').value,
                unit: document.getElementById('modalItemUnit').value.trim() || 'Pieces',
                initial_stock: document.getElementById('modalInitialStock').value,
                low_stock_threshold: document.getElementById('modalLowThreshold').value,
                unit_cost: document.getElementById('modalUnitCost').value,
                location: document.getElementById('modalLocation').value.trim(),
            };

            try {
                const res = await fetch('/inventory/api/items/create/', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message);
                    closeModal('addItemModal');
                    await fetchItems();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || 'Failed to create item', 'error');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
            } finally {
                btn.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        function openEditModal(itemId) {
            const item = allItems.find(i => i.id == itemId);
            if (!item) return;
            document.getElementById('editItemId').value = item.id;
            document.getElementById('editItemName').value = item.item_name;
            document.getElementById('editItemCategory').value = item.category;
            document.getElementById('editItemUnit').value = item.unit;
            document.getElementById('editLowThreshold').value = item.low_stock_threshold;
            document.getElementById('editUnitCost').value = item.unit_cost;
            document.getElementById('editLocation').value = item.location;
            openModal('editItemModal');
        }

        async function handleUpdateItem(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const btn = document.getElementById('editItemSubmitBtn');
            btn.disabled = true;
            const itemId = document.getElementById('editItemId').value;
            const payload = {
                item_name: document.getElementById('editItemName').value.trim(),
                category: document.getElementById('editItemCategory').value,
                unit: document.getElementById('editItemUnit').value.trim(),
                low_stock_threshold: document.getElementById('editLowThreshold').value,
                unit_cost: document.getElementById('editUnitCost').value,
                location: document.getElementById('editLocation').value.trim(),
            };

            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/inventory/api/items/${itemId}/update/`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message);
                    closeModal('editItemModal');
                    await fetchItems();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || 'Failed to update', 'error');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
            } finally {
                btn.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        async function deleteItem(itemId, itemName) {
            if (!confirm(`Delete '${itemName}' from inventory?`)) return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/inventory/api/items/${itemId}/delete/`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() } });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) { showToast(data.message); await fetchItems(); }
                else { showToast(data.error || 'Failed to delete', 'error'); }
            } catch (err) { showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error'); }
        }

        function openStockInModal(itemId, itemName, unit) {
            document.getElementById('quickInItemId').value = itemId;
            document.getElementById('quickInItemName').value = `${itemName} (${unit})`;
            document.getElementById('quickInQty').value = '';
            document.getElementById('quickInSource').value = '';
            openModal('quickStockInModal');
        }

        async function handleQuickStockIn(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const btn = document.getElementById('quickInSubmitBtn');
            btn.disabled = true;
            const payload = {
                item_id: document.getElementById('quickInItemId').value,
                quantity: document.getElementById('quickInQty').value,
                unit_cost: document.getElementById('quickInCost').value,
                source: document.getElementById('quickInSource').value.trim(),
            };
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/inventory/api/stock/in/', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) { showToast(data.message); closeModal('quickStockInModal'); await fetchItems(); }
                else { showToast(data.error || 'Failed Stock In', 'error'); }
            } catch (err) { showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error'); }
            finally { btn.disabled = false; }
        }

        function openStockOutModal(itemId, itemName, currentStock, unit) {
            document.getElementById('quickOutItemId').value = itemId;
            document.getElementById('quickOutItemName').value = itemName;
            document.getElementById('quickOutQty').value = '';
            document.getElementById('quickOutQty').max = currentStock;
            document.getElementById('quickOutMaxHint').innerText = `Max available: ${currentStock} ${unit}`;
            document.getElementById('quickOutDest').value = '';
            openModal('quickStockOutModal');
        }

        async function handleQuickStockOut(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const btn = document.getElementById('quickOutSubmitBtn');
            btn.disabled = true;
            const payload = {
                item_id: document.getElementById('quickOutItemId').value,
                quantity: document.getElementById('quickOutQty').value,
                destination: document.getElementById('quickOutDest').value.trim(),
                reference_number: document.getElementById('quickOutRef').value.trim(),
            };
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/inventory/api/stock/out/', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) { showToast(data.message); closeModal('quickStockOutModal'); await fetchItems(); }
                else { showToast(data.error || 'Failed Stock Out', 'error'); }
            } catch (err) { showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error'); }
            finally { btn.disabled = false; }
        }

        // =========================================================
        // MODULE 6: BOOK DISTRIBUTION (CLASS 1â€“10)
        // =========================================================
        async function loadBooksTab() {
            try {
                // Fetch eligible Class 1-10 students
                const resStudents = await fetch('/distributions/api/students/eligible/?benefit_type=BOOK');
                const stData = await resStudents.json();
                const stSelect = document.getElementById('bookStudentSelect');
                stSelect.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Choose Class 1-10 Student --</option>` + stData.students.map(s => `
                    <option value="${s.id}">${escapeHTML(s.name)} (${escapeHTML(s.current_class)}) - ${escapeHTML(s.school_name)}</option>
                `).join('');

                // Fetch Book inventory items
                const bookItems = allItems.filter(i => i.category === 'BOOKS');
                const itSelect = document.getElementById('bookItemSelect');
                itSelect.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Choose Book from Inventory --</option>` + bookItems.map(i => `
                    <option value="${i.id}">${escapeHTML(i.item_name)} (${escapeHTML(i.sku)}) - In Stock: ${i.current_stock} ${escapeHTML(i.unit)}</option>
                `).join('');

                // Fetch Book distribution history
                const resHist = await fetch('/distributions/api/history/?benefit_type=BOOK');
                const histData = await resHist.json();
                const tbody = document.getElementById('bookHistoryTableBody');
                if (histData.distributions.length === 0) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-light); padding: 30px;">No book distributions logged yet.</td></tr>`;
                } else {
                    tbody.innerHTML = histData.distributions.map(d => `
                        <tr>
                            <td>${d.distribution_date}</td>
                            <td><strong>${escapeHTML(d.student_name)}</strong></td>
                            <td><small style="font-family: monospace;">${escapeHTML(d.admission_number)}</small></td>
                            <td><span class="category-tag">${escapeHTML(d.student_class)}</span></td>
                            <td>${escapeHTML(d.school_name)}</td>
                            <td><strong>${escapeHTML(d.item_name)}</strong></td>
                            <td>${d.quantity}</td>
                            <td>${escapeHTML(d.issued_by)}</td>
                        </tr>
                    `).join('');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Error loading Books tab:', err);
            }
        }

        async function handleDistributeBook(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const btn = document.getElementById('bookDistSubmitBtn');
            btn.disabled = true;

            const payload = {
                student_id: document.getElementById('bookStudentSelect').value,
                item_id: document.getElementById('bookItemSelect').value,
                quantity: document.getElementById('bookQuantity').value,
                academic_year: document.getElementById('bookAcademicYear').value.trim(),
                remarks: document.getElementById('bookRemarks').value.trim(),
            };

            try {
                const res = await fetch('/distributions/api/distribute/book/', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message);
                    document.getElementById('bookDistForm').reset();
                    await fetchItems();
                    await loadBooksTab();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || 'Failed to distribute book', 'error');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
            } finally {
                btn.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        // =========================================================
        // MODULE 8: STUDY KIT MANAGEMENT (UP TO 2ND PUC)

                const kitData = await resKits.json();
                allStudyKits = kitData.kits;
                document.getElementById('kitTypeSelect').innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Choose Study Kit --</option>` + allStudyKits.map(k => `
                    <option value="${k.id}">${escapeHTML(k.name)} [${escapeHTML(k.target_grade_level)}]</option>
                `).join('');

                const kitItems = allItems.filter(i => i.category === 'STUDY_KITS');
                document.getElementById('kitInventoryItemSelect').innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Optional: Link Physical Inventory Item --</option>` + kitItems.map(i => `
                    <option value="${i.id}">${escapeHTML(i.item_name)} - In Stock: ${i.current_stock} ${escapeHTML(i.unit)}</option>
                `).join('');

                const resHist = await fetch('/distributions/api/history/?benefit_type=STUDY_KIT');
                const histData = await resHist.json();
                const tbody = document.getElementById('kitHistoryTableBody');
                if (histData.distributions.length === 0) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-light); padding: 30px;">No study kits issued yet.</td></tr>`;
                } else {
                    tbody.innerHTML = histData.distributions.map(d => `
                        <tr>
                            <td>${d.distribution_date}</td>
                            <td><strong>${escapeHTML(d.student_name)}</strong></td>
                            <td><span class="category-tag">${escapeHTML(d.student_class)}</span></td>
                            <td>${escapeHTML(d.school_name)}</td>
                            <td><strong>${escapeHTML(d.study_kit_name || d.item_name)}</strong></td>
                            <td>${d.quantity}</td>
                            <td>${escapeHTML(d.issued_by)}</td>
                        </tr>
                    `).join('');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Error loading Study Kits tab:', err);
            }
        }

        function showKitComponents() {
            const kitId = document.getElementById('kitTypeSelect').value;
            const kit = allStudyKits.find(k => k.id == kitId);
            const box = document.getElementById('kitComponentsBox');
            const list = document.getElementById('kitComponentsList');

            if (kit && kit.items && kit.items.length > 0) {
                list.innerHTML = kit.items.map(it => `<li>${it.quantity}x ${escapeHTML(it.item_name)} ${it.spec ? '(' + escapeHTML(it.spec) + ')' : ''}</li>`).join('');
                box.style.display = 'block';
            } else {
                box.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }

        function openCreateStudyKitModal() { document.getElementById('createStudyKitForm').reset(); openModal('createStudyKitModal'); }

            e.preventDefault();

            btn.disabled = true;

                name: document.getElementById('newKitName').value.trim(),
                target_grade_level: document.getElementById('newKitGrade').value.trim(),
                description: document.getElementById('newKitDesc').value.trim(),
            };

            try {
                const res = await fetch('/distributions/api/study-kits/', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) { showToast(data.message); closeModal('createStudyKitModal'); await loadStudyKitsTab(); }
                else { showToast(data.error || 'Failed to create kit', 'error'); }
            } catch (err) { showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error'); }
            finally { btn.disabled = false; }
        }

        // =========================================================
        // MODULE 9: LAPTOPS & TOP 10 PUC MERIT SELECTION
        // =========================================================
        async function loadLaptopsTab() {
            try {
                // Fetch Top 10 PUC Students
                const resTop = await fetch('/distributions/api/students/top-puc/');
                const topData = await resTop.json();
                const topTbody = document.getElementById('topPucTableBody');

                if (topData.top_students.length === 0) {
                    topTbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-light); padding: 30px;">No PUC students found in academic records.</td></tr>`;
                } else {
                    topTbody.innerHTML = topData.top_students.map(s => {
                        let rankClass = 'rank-normal';
                        if (s.rank_position === 1) rankClass = 'rank-gold';
                        else if (s.rank_position === 2) rankClass = 'rank-silver';
                        else if (s.rank_position === 3) rankClass = 'rank-bronze';

                        let statusCol = s.has_laptop
                            ? `<span class="status-badge" style="background: #eff6ff; color: #1d4ed8;">âœ… Laptop Issued (${escapeHTML(s.assigned_laptop)})</span>`
                            : `<span class="status-badge status-low-stock">â³ Eligible for Award</span>`;

                        let actionCol = s.has_laptop
                            ? `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" disabled>Assigned</button>`
                            : `<button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="openAwardLaptopModal('${s.student_id}', '${escapeJS(s.student_name)}')">ðŸŽ“ Award Laptop</button>`;

                        return `
                            <tr>
                                <td><span class="rank-badge ${rankClass}">#${s.rank_position}</span></td>
                                <td><strong>${escapeHTML(s.student_name)}</strong></td>
                                <td><small style="font-family: monospace;">${escapeHTML(s.admission_number)}</small></td>
                                <td><span class="category-tag LAPTOPS">${escapeHTML(s.current_class)}</span></td>
                                <td>${escapeHTML(s.school_name)}</td>
                                <td><strong style="color: #047857; font-size: 14.5px;">${s.percentage.toFixed(1)}%</strong></td>
                                <td>${statusCol}</td>
                                <td>${actionCol}</td>
                            </tr>
                        `;
                    }).join('');
                }

                // Fetch Serialized Laptops
                const resLaptops = await fetch('/inventory/api/laptops/');
                const lapData = await resLaptops.json();
                allLaptops = lapData.laptops;
                renderLaptopsTable(allLaptops);
                document.getElementById('kpiLaptops').innerText = allLaptops.length;
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Error loading Laptops tab:', err);
            }
        }

        function renderLaptopsTable(laptops) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('laptopsTableBody');
            if (!laptops || laptops.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-light); padding: 30px;">No laptop assets registered.</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            tbody.innerHTML = laptops.map(l => {
                let statusBadge = l.status === 'AVAILABLE'
                    ? `<span class="status-badge status-in-stock">Available</span>`
                    : (l.status === 'ISSUED' ? `<span class="status-badge" style="background: #eff6ff; color: #1d4ed8;">Issued</span>` : `<span class="status-badge status-out-stock">${escapeHTML(l.status_display)}</span>`);

                let assignmentInfo = l.current_assignment
                    ? `<strong>${escapeHTML(l.current_assignment.student_name)}</strong><br><small style="color: var(--text-light);">${l.current_assignment.academic_year}</small>`
                    : '<span style="color: var(--text-light);">-</span>';

                let actionBtn = l.current_assignment
                    ? `<button class="btn btn-warning btn-sm" onclick="returnLaptop('${l.id}', '${escapeJS(l.asset_number)}')">â†©ï¸ Return</button>`
                    : (l.status === 'AVAILABLE' ? `<button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="openAwardLaptopModal('', '', '${l.id}')">ðŸŽ“ Issue</button>` : '');

                return `
                    <tr>
                        <td><strong>${escapeHTML(l.asset_number)}</strong></td>
                        <td>${escapeHTML(l.brand)} ${escapeHTML(l.model_name)}</td>
                        <td><small style="font-family: monospace;">${escapeHTML(l.serial_number)}</small></td>
                        <td><span class="category-tag">${escapeHTML(l.condition_display)}</span></td>
                        <td>${statusBadge}</td>
                        <td>${assignmentInfo}</td>
                        <td>${actionBtn}</td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        function openAwardLaptopModal(studentId, studentName, preselectedLaptopId = '') {
            document.getElementById('awardStudentId').value = studentId;
            document.getElementById('awardStudentName').value = studentName || 'Merit Scholarship Student';

            const available = allLaptops.filter(l => l.status === 'AVAILABLE');
            const select = document.getElementById('awardLaptopSelect');
            select.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Choose Available Laptop --</option>` + available.map(l => `
                <option value="${l.id}" ${preselectedLaptopId == l.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${l.asset_number} - ${l.brand} ${l.model_name} (${l.serial_number})</option>
            `).join('');

            openModal('awardLaptopModal');
        }

        async function handleAwardLaptop(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const btn = document.getElementById('awardLaptopSubmitBtn');
            btn.disabled = true;

            const payload = {
                student_id: document.getElementById('awardStudentId').value,
                laptop_id: document.getElementById('awardLaptopSelect').value,
                academic_year: document.getElementById('awardAcademicYear').value.trim(),
                notes: document.getElementById('awardNotes').value.trim(),
            };

            try {
                const res = await fetch('/distributions/api/distribute/laptop/', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) {
                    showToast(data.message);
                    closeModal('awardLaptopModal');
                    await loadLaptopsTab();
                } else {
                    showToast(data.error || 'Failed to issue laptop', 'error');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
            } finally {
                btn.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        function openRegisterLaptopModal() { document.getElementById('registerLaptopForm').reset(); openModal('registerLaptopModal'); }

        async function handleRegisterLaptop(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const btn = document.getElementById('registerLaptopSubmitBtn');
            btn.disabled = true;
            const payload = {
                asset_number: document.getElementById('modalAssetNo').value.trim(),
                serial_number: document.getElementById('modalSerialNo').value.trim(),
                brand: document.getElementById('modalBrand').value.trim(),
                model_name: document.getElementById('modalModel').value.trim(),
                purchase_cost: document.getElementById('modalPurchaseCost').value,
                condition: document.getElementById('modalCondition').value,
            };
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/inventory/api/laptops/create/', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() },
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) { showToast(data.message); closeModal('registerLaptopModal'); await loadLaptopsTab(); }
                else { showToast(data.error || 'Failed to register', 'error'); }
            } catch (err) { showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error'); }
            finally { btn.disabled = false; }
        }

        async function returnLaptop(laptopId, assetNo) {
            const notes = prompt(`Enter return notes for laptop ${assetNo}:`, 'Returned in good working condition');
            if (notes === null) return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/inventory/api/laptops/${laptopId}/return/`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ return_notes: notes })
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) { showToast(data.message); await loadLaptopsTab(); }
                else { showToast(data.error || 'Failed to return', 'error'); }
            } catch (err) { showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error'); }
        }

        // =========================================================
        // MODULE 12: GOVERNMENT SCHOOL ESSENTIALS (11 CATEGORIES)
        // =========================================================

                // Fetch schools

                `).join('');

                filterEssentialsInventoryDropdown();

                // Fetch history
                const resHist = await fetch('/distributions/api/history/?benefit_type=SCHOOL_ESSENTIAL');
                const histData = await resHist.json();
                const tbody = document.getElementById('essentialsHistoryTableBody');
                if (histData.distributions.length === 0) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-light); padding: 30px;">No school essentials allocated yet.</td></tr>`;
                } else {
                    tbody.innerHTML = histData.distributions.map(d => `
                        <tr>
                            <td>${d.distribution_date}</td>

        }

        // =========================================================
        // LOW STOCK ALERTS & LEDGER (TAB 7 & 8)
        // =========================================================
        async function fetchLowStockAlerts() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/inventory/api/alerts/low-stock/');
                const data = await res.json();
                const tbody = document.getElementById('alertsTableBody');
                if (data.alerts.length === 0) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align: center; color: var(--success-color); padding: 40px; font-weight: 600;">ðŸŽ‰ All inventory items have healthy stock levels above minimum thresholds.</td></tr>`;
                    return;
                }
                tbody.innerHTML = data.alerts.map(item => {
                    const shortage = Math.max(0, item.low_stock_threshold - item.current_stock);
                    const recommended = shortage > 0 ? shortage + 20 : 25;
                    return `
                        <tr>
                            <td><strong>${escapeHTML(item.item_name)}</strong><br><small style="color: var(--text-light); font-family: monospace;">${escapeHTML(item.sku)}</small></td>
                            <td><span class="category-tag ${item.category}">${escapeHTML(item.category_display)}</span></td>
                            <td><strong style="color: var(--danger-color); font-size: 15px;">${item.current_stock} ${escapeHTML(item.unit)}</strong></td>
                            <td>${item.low_stock_threshold} ${escapeHTML(item.unit)}</td>
                            <td><strong style="color: #d97706;">+${recommended} ${escapeHTML(item.unit)}</strong></td>
                            <td>${item.current_stock === 0 ? '<span class="status-badge status-out-stock">Out of Stock</span>' : '<span class="status-badge status-low-stock">Low Stock</span>'}</td>
                            <td><button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="openStockInModal('${item.id}', '${escapeJS(item.item_name)}', '${escapeJS(item.unit)}')">ðŸ“¥ Quick Restock</button></td>
                        </tr>
                    `;
                }).join('');
            } catch (err) { console.error('Error alerts:', err); }
        }

        async function fetchLedgerTransactions() {
            const type = document.getElementById('ledgerTypeFilter').value;
            let url = '/inventory/api/transactions/';
            if (type) url += `?type=${type}`;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(url);
                const data = await res.json();
                const tbody = document.getElementById('ledgerTableBody');
                if (data.transactions.length === 0) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-light); padding: 30px;">No movement transactions logged.</td></tr>`;
                    return;
                }
                tbody.innerHTML = data.transactions.map(tx => {
                    let typeBadge = tx.transaction_type === 'STOCK_IN' ? '<span class="tx-type-badge tx-type-in">ðŸ“¥ Stock In</span>' : (tx.transaction_type === 'STOCK_OUT' ? '<span class="tx-type-badge tx-type-out">ðŸ“¤ Stock Out</span>' : '<span class="tx-type-badge tx-type-adj">âš™ï¸ Adjustment</span>');
                    let color = tx.transaction_type === 'STOCK_IN' ? 'var(--success-color)' : 'var(--danger-color)';
                    let sign = tx.transaction_type === 'STOCK_IN' ? '+' : '-';
                    return `
                        <tr>
                            <td>${escapeHTML(tx.created_at)}</td>
                            <td><strong>${escapeHTML(tx.item_name)}</strong><br><small style="color: var(--text-light);">${escapeHTML(tx.item_sku)}</small></td>
                            <td>${typeBadge}</td>
                            <td><strong style="color: ${color};">${sign}${tx.quantity} ${escapeHTML(tx.item_unit)}</strong></td>
                            <td>${tx.previous_stock} âž” <strong>${tx.new_stock}</strong></td>
                            <td>${escapeHTML(tx.source_destination)}</td>
                            <td><small style="font-family: monospace;">${escapeHTML(tx.reference_number)}</small></td>
                            <td>${escapeHTML(tx.performed_by)}</td>
                        </tr>
                    `;
                }).join('');
            } catch (err) { console.error('Error ledger:', err); }
        }

        // =========================================================
        // TAB 9: REPORTS HUB
        // =========================================================
        async function loadReportsTab() {
            try {
                const resSch = await fetch('/distributions/api/reports/school-wise/');
                const schData = await resSch.json();
                document.getElementById('schoolReportTableBody').innerHTML = schData.report.map(s => `
                    <tr>
                        <td><strong>${escapeHTML(s.school_name)}</strong></td>
                        <td><small style="font-family: monospace;">${escapeHTML(s.udise_code)}</small></td>
                        <td>${escapeHTML(s.district)}</td>
                        <td><strong>${s.books_distributed}</strong></td>
                        <td><strong>${s.workbooks_distributed}</strong></td>
                        <td><strong>${s.study_kits_distributed}</strong></td>
                        <td><strong>${s.laptops_issued}</strong></td>
                        <td><strong>${s.school_essentials_units}</strong></td>
                        <td><strong style="color: var(--primary-color); font-size: 15px;">${s.total_items_delivered}</strong></td>
                    </tr>
                `).join('');

                const resSt = await fetch('/distributions/api/reports/student-wise/');
                const stData = await resSt.json();
                document.getElementById('studentReportTableBody').innerHTML = stData.report.map(s => `
                    <tr>
                        <td><strong>${escapeHTML(s.student_name)}</strong></td>
                        <td><small style="font-family: monospace;">${escapeHTML(s.admission_number)}</small></td>
                        <td><span class="category-tag">${escapeHTML(s.current_class)}</span></td>
                        <td>${escapeHTML(s.school_name)}</td>
                        <td>${s.books_received}</td>
                        <td>${s.workbooks_received}</td>
                        <td>${s.study_kits_received}</td>
                        <td>${s.laptops_received}</td>
                        <td><strong style="color: var(--success-color); font-size: 15px;">${s.total_benefits}</strong></td>
                    </tr>
                `).join('');
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Error loading reports:', err);
            }
        }

        function escapeHTML(str) {
            if (!str) return '';
            return String(str).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
        }

        function escapeJS(str) {
            if (!str) return '';
            return String(str).replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/"/g, '\\"');
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            fetchItems();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
// Global State
let currentSchoolId = "{{ selected_school.id|default:'' }}" || null;
let currentSchoolData = null;

// Get CSRF Token for Django POST requests
function getCSRFToken() {
    const input = document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]');
    if (input) return input.value;
    const cookieValue = document.cookie
        .split('; ')
        .find(row => row.startsWith('csrftoken='))
        ?.split('=')[1];
    return cookieValue || '';
}

// Toast notification helper
function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
    const container = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    const toast = document.createElement('div');
    toast.className = `toast toast-${type}`;
    const icon = type === 'success' ? 'âœ…' : (type === 'error' ? 'âŒ' : 'â„¹ï¸');
    toast.innerHTML = `<span>${icon}</span> <span>${message}</span>`;
    container.appendChild(toast);
    setTimeout(() => {
        toast.style.opacity = '0';
        toast.style.transform = 'translateY(10px)';
        toast.style.transition = 'all 0.3s ease';
        setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
    }, 3500);
}

// Handle Login Form Submission
async function handleLoginSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const submitBtn = document.getElementById('loginSubmitBtn');
    const username = document.getElementById('username').value.trim();
    const password = document.getElementById('password').value.trim();

    if (!username || !password) {
        showToast('Please enter both username and password.', 'error');
        return;
    }

    submitBtn.disabled = true;
    submitBtn.innerText = 'Verifying...';

    try {
        const response = await fetch('/schools/api/auth/login/', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken()
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({ username, password })
        });
        const result = await response.json();

        if (result.success) {
            showToast(result.message || 'Login successful!');
            document.getElementById('login-module').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('dashboard-content').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('userBadgeContainer').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (result.user && result.user.username) {
                document.getElementById('displayUsername').innerText = result.user.username;
            }
            if (currentSchoolId) {
                fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
            }
        } else {
            showToast(result.error || 'Invalid credentials', 'error');
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Login request failed: ' + err.message, 'error');
    } finally {
        submitBtn.disabled = false;
        submitBtn.innerText = 'Login to Dashboard';
    }
}

// Handle Logout
async function handleLogout() {
    try {
        await fetch('/schools/api/auth/logout/', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() }
        });
    } catch (e) {}
    document.getElementById('dashboard-content').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('login-module').classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('userBadgeContainer').classList.add('hidden');
    showToast('Logged out successfully', 'info');
}

// Tab navigation logic
function activateTab(tabName) {
    const contents = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content');
    contents.forEach((c) => c.classList.remove('active'));
    
    const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-button');
    buttons.forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('active'));
    
    const activeContent = document.getElementById(tabName);
    if (activeContent) {
        activeContent.classList.add('active');
    }
    const activeButton = document.querySelector(`.tab-button[data-tab="${tabName}"]`);
    if (activeButton) {
        activeButton.classList.add('active');
    }
}

// Attach click listeners to tab buttons
document.querySelectorAll('.tab-button').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const tab = btn.getAttribute('data-tab');
        if (tab) activateTab(tab);
    });
});

// Fetch complete school details and populate all tabs
async function fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(schoolId) {
    if (!schoolId) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/schools/api/schools/${schoolId}/`);
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
            currentSchoolId = schoolId;
            currentSchoolData = data;
            
            // Update Banner
            const school = data.school;
            const loc = school.village || school.district || 'General';
            document.getElementById('bannerSchoolLocation').innerText = `ðŸ“ ${loc}`;
            document.getElementById('bannerSchoolAffiliation').innerText = `ðŸ“œ ${school.affiliation || 'State Board'}`;
            document.getElementById('bannerSchoolStudents').innerText = `ðŸ§‘â€ðŸŽ“ ${school.student_strength} Students`;

            // Populate Edit Form
            document.getElementById('editSchoolName').value = school.name || '';
            document.getElementById('editPrincipal').value = school.headmaster_name || '';
            document.getElementById('editAffiliation').value = school.affiliation || 'State';

            // Populate Contact Form
            document.getElementById('phone').value = school.phone || '';
            document.getElementById('email').value = school.email || '';
            document.getElementById('website').value = school.website || '';

            // Populate Headmaster Form
            document.getElementById('headmasterName').value = school.headmaster_name || '';
            document.getElementById('headmasterQualification').value = school.headmaster_qualification || '';
            document.getElementById('headmasterExperience').value = school.headmaster_experience !== null ? school.headmaster_experience : '';
            
            const previewBox = document.getElementById('headmasterPhotoPreview');
            if (school.headmaster_photo_url) {
                previewBox.innerHTML = `<img src="${school.headmaster_photo_url}" alt="Headmaster Photo">`;
            } else {
                previewBox.innerHTML = `<span style="font-size: 26px; color: #94a3b8;">ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ«</span>`;
            }

            // Populate Student Strength Table
            renderStudentStrengthTable(data.strengths, school.student_strength);

            // Populate Milestones History
            renderMilestones(data.milestones);

            // Populate Resources
            renderResources(data.resources);
        }
    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error fetching school data:", err);
    }
}

// On School Dropdown Change
function onSelectedSchoolChange(schoolId) {
    currentSchoolId = schoolId;
    fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(schoolId);
}

// Render Student Strength Table
function renderStudentStrengthTable(strengths, totalStrength) {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('studentStrengthTableBody');
    const tfoot = document.getElementById('studentStrengthTableFoot');
    if (!tbody) return;

    if (!strengths || strengths.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align: center; color: #94a3b8; padding: 20px;">No grade records available.</td></tr>`;
        tfoot.innerHTML = '';
        return;
    }

    let maleSum = 0;
    let femaleSum = 0;
    let totalSum = 0;

    tbody.innerHTML = strengths.map(row => {
        maleSum += (row.male_students || 0);
        femaleSum += (row.female_students || 0);
        totalSum += (row.total_students || 0);

        let changeClass = 'change-neutral';
        const ch = (row.change_vs_last_year || '0').trim();
        if (ch.startsWith('+')) changeClass = 'change-positive';
        else if (ch.startsWith('-')) changeClass = 'change-negative';

        return `
            <tr>
                <td><strong>${row.grade_level}</strong></td>
                <td>${row.male_students}</td>
                <td>${row.female_students}</td>
                <td><strong>${row.total_students}</strong></td>
                <td><span class="change-pill ${changeClass}">${row.change_vs_last_year || '0'}</span></td>
            </tr>
        `;
    }).join('');

    tfoot.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
            <td><strong>Total Strength</strong></td>
            <td><strong>${maleSum}</strong></td>
            <td><strong>${femaleSum}</strong></td>
            <td><strong>${totalSum || totalStrength}</strong></td>
            <td>-</td>
        </tr>
    `;
}

// Render Milestones
function renderMilestones(milestones) {
    const container = document.getElementById('historyMilestonesContainer');
    if (!container) return;

    if (!milestones || milestones.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `<div style="padding: 20px; text-align: center; color: #94a3b8; background: #fff; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px dashed #cbd5e1;">No historical milestones recorded yet. Click "Add Milestone" to create one.</div>`;
        return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = milestones.map(m => `
        <div class="info-card">
            <div class="info-card-header">
                <div class="info-card-title">ðŸ“Œ Milestone: ${m.title} ${m.year_or_date ? `(${m.year_or_date})` : ''}</div>
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="padding: 3px 8px; font-size: 11px; background: #ef4444;" onclick="deleteMilestone(${m.id})">Delete</button>
            </div>
            <div class="info-card-body">
                <strong>Details:</strong> ${m.details || 'N/A'}<br>
                <strong>Impact:</strong> ${m.impact || 'N/A'}
            </div>
        </div>
    `).join('');
}

// Render Resources
function renderResources(resources) {
    const container = document.getElementById('resourcesContainer');
    if (!container) return;

    if (!resources || resources.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `<div style="padding: 20px; text-align: center; color: #94a3b8; background: #fff; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px dashed #cbd5e1;">No allocated resources logged yet. Click "Allocate Resource" to add.</div>`;
        return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = resources.map(r => {
        let stClass = 'active';
        const st = (r.status || 'Active').toLowerCase();
        if (st.includes('deliver')) stClass = 'delivered';
        else if (st.includes('progress')) stClass = 'in-progress';
        else if (st.includes('pend')) stClass = 'pending';

        return `
            <div class="info-card">
                <div class="info-card-header">
                    <div class="info-card-title">ðŸ“¦ Resource: ${r.resource_name}</div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center;">
                        <span class="status-badge ${stClass}">${r.status}</span>
                        <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="padding: 3px 8px; font-size: 11px; background: #ef4444;" onclick="deleteResource(${r.id})">Delete</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="info-card-body">
                    <strong>Status:</strong> ${r.status}<br>
                    <strong>Quantity:</strong> ${r.quantity || 1}<br>
                    ${r.details ? `<strong>Details:</strong> ${r.details}<br>` : ''}
                    <strong>Last Updated:</strong> ${r.last_updated_note || 'Recent'}
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    }).join('');
}

// 1. Handle Add School (Tab 1)
async function handleAddSchool(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const btn = document.getElementById('addSchoolSubmitBtn');
    btn.disabled = true;

    const payload = {
        name: document.getElementById('schoolName').value.trim(),
        udise_code: document.getElementById('udiseCode').value.trim(),
        location: document.getElementById('location').value.trim(),
        state: document.getElementById('state').value.trim(),
        admissionDate: document.getElementById('admissionDate').value,
        affiliation: document.getElementById('addAffiliation').value,
    };

    try {
        const res = await fetch('/schools/api/schools/create/', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken()
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
            showToast(data.message || 'School added successfully!');
            document.getElementById('addSchoolForm').reset();
            
            // Add to dropdown and select it
            const select = document.getElementById('activeSchoolSelect');
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = data.school.id;
            opt.text = `${data.school.name} (UDISE: ${data.school.udise_code})`;
            select.appendChild(opt);
            select.value = data.school.id;
            
            currentSchoolId = data.school.id;
            fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
            activateTab('edit-details');
        } else {
            showToast(data.error || 'Failed to add school', 'error');
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
    }
}

// 2. Handle Edit School Details (Tab 2)
async function handleEditSchool(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!currentSchoolId) {
        showToast('No school selected.', 'error');
        return;
    }

    const btn = document.getElementById('editSchoolSubmitBtn');
    btn.disabled = true;

    const payload = {
        name: document.getElementById('editSchoolName').value.trim(),
        principal: document.getElementById('editPrincipal').value.trim(),
        affiliation: document.getElementById('editAffiliation').value
    };

    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/schools/api/schools/${currentSchoolId}/edit/`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken()
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
            showToast(data.message || 'School details updated!');
            const select = document.getElementById('activeSchoolSelect');
            const opt = select.querySelector(`option[value="${currentSchoolId}"]`);
            if (opt) opt.text = `${data.school.name} (UDISE: ${data.school.udise_code})`;
            fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
        } else {
            showToast(data.error || 'Failed to update details', 'error');
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
    }
}

// 3. Handle Save Contact Info (Tab 3)
async function handleSaveContactInfo(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!currentSchoolId) {
        showToast('No school selected.', 'error');
        return;
    }

    const btn = document.getElementById('saveContactSubmitBtn');
    btn.disabled = true;

    const payload = {
        phone: document.getElementById('phone').value.trim(),
        email: document.getElementById('email').value.trim(),
        website: document.getElementById('website').value.trim()
    };

    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/schools/api/schools/${currentSchoolId}/contact/`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken()
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
            showToast(data.message || 'Contact details saved!');
        } else {
            showToast(data.error || 'Failed to save contact details', 'error');
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
    }
}

// 4. Handle Save Headmaster Details (Tab 4)
async function handleSaveHeadmasterDetails(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!currentSchoolId) {
        showToast('No school selected.', 'error');
        return;
    }

    const btn = document.getElementById('saveHeadmasterSubmitBtn');
    btn.disabled = true;

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('headmasterName', document.getElementById('headmasterName').value.trim());
    formData.append('headmasterQualification', document.getElementById('headmasterQualification').value.trim());
    formData.append('headmasterExperience', document.getElementById('headmasterExperience').value.trim());

    const photoInput = document.getElementById('headmasterPhoto');
    if (photoInput.files && photoInput.files[0]) {
        formData.append('headmasterPhoto', photoInput.files[0]);
    }

    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/schools/api/schools/${currentSchoolId}/headmaster/`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken()
            },
            body: formData
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
            showToast(data.message || 'Headmaster profile updated!');
            if (data.photo_url) {
                document.getElementById('headmasterPhotoPreview').innerHTML = `<img src="${data.photo_url}" alt="Headmaster Photo">`;
            }
            fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
        } else {
            showToast(data.error || 'Failed to update profile', 'error');
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
    }
}

// Preview Headmaster Photo before upload
function previewHeadmasterPhoto(input) {
    if (input.files && input.files[0]) {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = function(e) {
            document.getElementById('headmasterPhotoPreview').innerHTML = `<img src="${e.target.result}" alt="Preview">`;
        }
        reader.readAsDataURL(input.files[0]);
    }
}

// 5. Export CSV
function exportStudentStrengthCSV() {
    if (!currentSchoolId) {
        window.location.href = '/schools/export/student-strength/';
    } else {
        window.location.href = `/schools/export/student-strength/${currentSchoolId}/`;
    }
    showToast('Exporting student strength CSV...', 'info');
}

// 6. Milestones Modals and Handlers
function openAddMilestoneModal() {
    if (!currentSchoolId) {
        showToast('Please select a school first', 'error');
        return;
    }
    document.getElementById('addMilestoneForm').reset();
    document.getElementById('addMilestoneModal').classList.add('open');
}

async function handleAddMilestone(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const payload = {
        title: document.getElementById('milestoneTitle').value.trim(),
        year_or_date: document.getElementById('milestoneYear').value.trim(),
        details: document.getElementById('milestoneDetails').value.trim(),
        impact: document.getElementById('milestoneImpact').value.trim()
    };

    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/schools/api/schools/${currentSchoolId}/milestones/add/`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken()
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
            showToast(data.message || 'Milestone added!');
            closeModal('addMilestoneModal');
            fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
        } else {
            showToast(data.error || 'Failed to add milestone', 'error');
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
    }
}

async function deleteMilestone(id) {
    if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this milestone record?')) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/schools/api/milestones/${id}/delete/`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() }
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
            showToast(data.message || 'Milestone deleted.');
            fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
    }
}

// 7. Resource Modals and Handlers
function openAddResourceModal() {
    if (!currentSchoolId) {
        showToast('Please select a school first', 'error');
        return;
    }
    document.getElementById('addResourceForm').reset();
    document.getElementById('addResourceModal').classList.add('open');
}

async function handleAddResource(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const payload = {
        resource_name: document.getElementById('resourceName').value.trim(),
        status: document.getElementById('resourceStatus').value,
        quantity: document.getElementById('resourceQuantity').value,
        last_updated_note: document.getElementById('resourceLastUpdated').value.trim(),
        details: document.getElementById('resourceDetails').value.trim()
    };

    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/schools/api/schools/${currentSchoolId}/resources/add/`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken()
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
            showToast(data.message || 'Resource allocated!');
            closeModal('addResourceModal');
            fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
        } else {
            showToast(data.error || 'Failed to allocate resource', 'error');
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
    }
}

async function deleteResource(id) {
    if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this allocated resource record?')) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/schools/api/resources/${id}/delete/`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': getCSRFToken() }
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.success) {
            showToast(data.message || 'Resource removed.');
            fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
    }
}

// Modal helper
function closeModal(id) {
    const modal = document.getElementById(id);
    if (modal) modal.classList.remove('open');
}

// Reset Form helper
function resetForm(formId) {
    const form = document.getElementById(formId);
    if (form) form.reset();
}

function loadSchoolDataIntoForms() {
    if (currentSchoolId) {
        fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
    }
}

// Initial setup
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    if (currentSchoolId) {
        fetchAndPopulateSchoolData(currentSchoolId);
    }
});

                Total Volunteers
            </div>

            <div class="stat-value">
                {{ volunteers|length }}
            </div>

        </div>


        <div class="stat-card">

            <div class="stat-label">
                Active Volunteers
            </div>

            <div class="stat-value">
                {{ active_count }}
            </div>

        </div>


        <div class="stat-card">

            <div class="stat-label">
                Inactive Volunteers
            </div>

            <div class="stat-value">
                {{ inactive_count }}
            </div>

        </div>

    </div>


    <!-- VOLUNTEER TABLE -->

    <div class="table-card">

        <div class="table-header">

            <h2>
                Registered Volunteers
            </h2>

            <input
                type="text"
                id="searchInput"
                class="search-box"
                placeholder="Search volunteers..."
                onkeyup="searchVolunteers()"
            >

        </div>


        <div class="table-wrapper">

            {% if volunteers %}

            <table id="volunteerTable">

                <thead>

                    <tr>

                        <th>
                            Name
                        </th>

                        <th>
                            Email
                        </th>

                        <th>
                            Phone
                        </th>

                        <th>
                            Occupation
                        </th>

                        <th>
                            Skills
                        </th>

                        <th>
                            Status
                        </th>

                        <th>
                            Action
                        </th>

                    </tr>

                </thead>


                <tbody>

                {% for volunteer in volunteers %}

                    <tr>

                        <td>

                            <div class="volunteer-name">
                                {{ volunteer.first_name }}
                                {{ volunteer.last_name }}
                            </div>

                        </td>


                        <td>

                            <span class="email">
                                {{ volunteer.email }}
                            </span>

                        </td>


                        <td>
                            {{ volunteer.phone }}
                        </td>


                        <td>

                            {% if volunteer.occupation %}
                                {{ volunteer.occupation }}
                            {% else %}
                                -
                            {% endif %}

                        </td>


                        <td>

                            {% if volunteer.skills %}
                                {{ volunteer.skills|truncatechars:35 }}
                            {% else %}
                                -
                            {% endif %}

                        </td>


                        <td>

                            {% if volunteer.status == "ACTIVE" %}

                                <span class="status status-active">
                                    Active
                                </span>

                            {% else %}

                                <span class="status status-inactive">
                                    Inactive
                                </span>

                            {% endif %}

                        </td>


                        <td>

                            <a
                                href="{% url 'volunteers:detail' volunteer.pk %}"
                                class="action-link"
                            >
                                View Details
                            </a>

                        </td>

                    </tr>

                {% endfor %}

                </tbody>

            </table>


            {% else %}

            <div class="empty">

                <h3>
                    No volunteers registered
                </h3>

                <p>
                    Start by adding your first volunteer.
                </p>

                <a
                    href="{% url 'volunteers:create' %}"
                    class="btn btn-primary"
                >
                    Add Volunteer
                </a>

            </div>

            {% endif %}

        </div>

    </div>


</main>


<script>

function searchVolunteers() {

    const input =
        document.getElementById("searchInput").value.toLowerCase();

    const rows =
        document.querySelectorAll("#volunteerTable tbody tr");

    rows.forEach(function(row) {

        const text =
            row.textContent.toLowerCase();

        if (text.includes(input)) {

            row.style.display = "";

        } else {

            row.style.display = "none";

        }

    });

}

</script>


</div>

</div>

</body>

</html>
